===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 516-538
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An r-value in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
516 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
517 |       switch (n.getType()) {
518 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
519 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
520 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
521 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
522 |         case Token.OR:
523 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
524 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
525 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
526 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
527 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
528 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
529 |         case Token.HOOK:
530 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
531 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
532 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
533 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
534 |           Node third = second.getNext();
535 |           return getValueType(third);
536 |       }
537 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
538 |     }

1015 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1016 |       throws IOException {

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1165-1185
Documentation:

Method code:
1165 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1166 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1167 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1168 |       return s;
1169 |     }
1170 | 
1171 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1172 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1173 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1174 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1175 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1176 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1177 |       // crazy.
1178 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1179 |         sb.append(c);
1180 |       } else {
1181 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1182 |       }
1183 |     }
1184 |     return sb.toString();
1185 |   }

===== M0035 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 197-220
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
197 |   boolean parse() {
198 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
199 |     skipEOLs();
200 | 
201 |     JsDocToken token = next();
202 | 
203 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
204 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
205 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
206 |       token = blockInfo.token;
207 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
212 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
213 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
214 |         // what it was.
215 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
216 |       }
217 |     }
218 | 
219 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
220 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 514-542
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     void endFile() {
516 |       super.endFile();
517 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
518 |         return;
519 |       }
520 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
521 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
522 |         append(";");
523 |         startNewLine();
524 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
525 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
526 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
527 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
528 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
529 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
530 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
531 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
532 |         lineIndex--;
533 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
534 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
535 |         append(";");
536 |         startNewLine();
537 |       } else {
538 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
539 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
540 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
541 |       }
542 |     }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.

===== M0058 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String,boolean,Name.Type)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 552-573
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @param isPropAssign Whether this set corresponds to a property
     *     assignment of the form <code>a.b.c = ...;</code>
     * @param type The type of the value that the name is being assigned
     */
Method code:
552 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name,
553 |                              boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {
554 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
555 | 
556 |       Name nameObj = getOrCreateName(name);
557 |       nameObj.type = type;
558 | 
559 |       Ref set = new Ref(t, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL,
560 |           currentPreOrderIndex++);
561 |       nameObj.addRef(set);
562 | 
563 |       if (isNestedAssign(parent)) {
564 |         // This assignment is both a set and a get that creates an alias.
565 |         Ref get = new Ref(t, n, nameObj, Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET,
566 |             currentPreOrderIndex++);
567 |         nameObj.addRef(get);
568 |         Ref.markTwins(set, get);
569 |       } else if (isTypeDeclaration(n, parent)) {
570 |         // Names with a @constructor or @enum annotation are always collapsed
571 |         nameObj.setDeclaredType();
572 |       }
573 |     }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 421-438
Documentation:
/**
     * @return Whether the variable is only assigned a value once for its
     *     lifetime.
     */
Method code:
421 |     boolean isAssignedOnceInLifetime() {
422 |       Reference ref = getOneAndOnlyAssignment();
423 |       if (ref == null) {
424 |         return false;
425 |       }
426 | 
427 |       // Make sure this assignment is not in a loop.
428 |       for (BasicBlock block = ref.getBasicBlock();
429 |            block != null; block = block.getParent()) {
430 |         if (block.isFunction) {
431 |           break;
432 |         } else if (block.isLoop) {
433 |           return false;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 | 
437 |       return true;
438 |     }

===== M0063 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

1755 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {

1757 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 567-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private void extractForInitializer(
568 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
569 | 
570 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
571 |         next = c.getNext();
572 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
573 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
574 |         switch (c.getType()) {
575 |           case Token.LABEL:
576 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
577 |             break;
578 |           case Token.FOR:
579 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
580 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
581 |               if (first.isVar()) {
582 |                 // Transform:
583 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
584 |                 // to:
585 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
586 |                 Node newStatement = first;
587 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
588 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
589 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
590 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
591 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
592 |               }
593 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
594 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
595 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
596 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
597 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
598 | 
599 |               Node newStatement;
600 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
601 |               // but are handled differently.
602 |               if (init.isVar()) {
603 |                 newStatement = init;
604 |               } else {
605 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
606 |               }
607 | 
608 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
609 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
610 |             }
611 |             break;
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(Name,String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 621-648
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name.
   * This involves flattening the global name (if it's not just a global
   * variable name already), collapsing object literal keys into global
   * variables, declaring stub global variables for properties added later
   * in a local scope.
   *
   * It may seem odd that this function also takes care of declaring stubs
   * for direct children. The ultimate goal of this function is to eliminate
   * the global name entirely (when possible), so that "middlemen" namespaces
   * disappear, and to do that we need to make sure that all the direct children
   * will be collapsed as well.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n} (e.g. "a", "a$b$c")
   * @param canCollapseChildNames Whether it's possible to collapse children of
   *     this name. (This is mostly passed for convenience; it's equivalent to
   *     n.canCollapseChildNames()).
   */
Method code:
621 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(
622 |       Name n, String alias, boolean canCollapseChildNames) {
623 |     Ref decl = n.getDeclaration();
624 |     if (decl == null) {
625 |       // Some names do not have declarations, because they
626 |       // are only defined in local scopes.
627 |       return;
628 |     }
629 | 
630 |     if (decl.getTwin() != null) {
631 |       // Twin declarations will get handled when normal references
632 |       // are handled.
633 |       return;
634 |     }
635 | 
636 |     switch (decl.node.getParent().getType()) {
637 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
638 |         updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(
639 |             n, alias, canCollapseChildNames);
640 |         break;
641 |       case Token.VAR:
642 |         updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtVarNode(n, canCollapseChildNames);
643 |         break;
644 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
645 |         updateFunctionDeclarationAtFunctionNode(n, canCollapseChildNames);
646 |         break;
647 |     }
648 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 310-438
Documentation:

Method code:
310 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
311 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
312 |         return;
313 |       }
314 | 
315 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
316 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
317 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
318 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
319 |       }
320 | 
321 |       String name;
322 |       boolean isSet = false;
323 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
324 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
325 | 
326 |       switch (n.getType()) {
327 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
328 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
329 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
330 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
331 |           name = null;
332 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
333 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
334 |           }
335 |           if (name == null) return;
336 |           isSet = true;
337 |           switch (n.getType()) {
338 |             case Token.STRING_KEY:
339 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
340 |               break;
341 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
342 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
343 |               break;
344 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
345 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
346 |               break;
347 |             default:
348 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
349 |           }
350 |           break;
351 |         case Token.NAME:
352 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
353 |           if (parent != null) {
354 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
355 |               case Token.VAR:
356 |                 isSet = true;
357 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
358 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
359 |                 break;
360 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
361 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
362 |                   isSet = true;
363 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
364 |                 }
365 |                 break;
366 |               case Token.GETPROP:
367 |                 return;
368 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
369 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
370 |                 if (gramps == null ||
371 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
372 |                 isSet = true;
373 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
374 |                 break;
375 |               case Token.INC:
376 |               case Token.DEC:
377 |                 isSet = true;
378 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
379 |                 break;
380 |               default:
381 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
382 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
383 |                   isSet = true;
384 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
385 |                 }
386 |             }
387 |           }
388 |           name = n.getString();
389 |           break;
390 |         case Token.GETPROP:
391 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
392 |           if (parent != null) {
393 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
394 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
395 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
396 |                   isSet = true;
397 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
398 |                   isPropAssign = true;
399 |                 }
400 |                 break;
401 |               case Token.INC:
402 |               case Token.DEC:
403 |                 isSet = true;
404 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
405 |                 break;
406 |               case Token.GETPROP:
407 |                 return;
408 |               default:
409 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
410 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
411 |                   isSet = true;
412 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
413 |                 }
414 |             }
415 |           }
416 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
417 |           if (name == null) return;
418 |           break;
419 |         default:
420 |           return;
421 |       }
422 | 
423 |       // We are only interested in global names.
424 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
425 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
426 |         return;
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       if (isSet) {
430 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
431 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
432 |         } else {
433 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
434 |         }
435 |       } else {
436 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
437 |       }
438 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.addGuard(WarningsGuard)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 87-102
Documentation:

Method code:
87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {
97 |       numberOfAdds++;
98 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
99 |       guards.remove(guard);
100 |       guards.add(guard);
101 |     }
102 |   }

384 |     @Override
385 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
386 |       switch (n.getType()) {
387 |         case Token.WHILE:
388 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
389 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
390 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
391 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
392 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);

394 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
395 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
396 |           }
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
400 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
401 |           break;
402 | 
403 |         case Token.NAME:
404 |         case Token.STRING:
405 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
406 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
407 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:

409 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n);
118 |     }
119 |   }

154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
155 |     switch (n.getType()) {
156 |       // Childless expressions
157 |       case Token.FALSE:
158 |       case Token.NULL:
159 |       case Token.THIS:
160 |       case Token.TRUE:
161 |         validateChildless(n);
162 |         return;
163 | 
164 |       // General unary ops
165 |       case Token.DELPROP:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.NEG:
168 |       case Token.NOT:
169 |       case Token.INC:
170 |       case Token.DEC:
171 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
172 |       case Token.VOID:
173 |       case Token.BITNOT:
174 |       case Token.CAST:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:
183 |       case Token.BITXOR:
184 |       case Token.BITAND:
185 |       case Token.EQ:
186 |       case Token.NE:
187 |       case Token.SHEQ:
188 |       case Token.SHNE:
189 |       case Token.LT:
190 |       case Token.GT:
191 |       case Token.LE:
192 |       case Token.GE:
193 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
194 |       case Token.IN:
195 |       case Token.LSH:
196 |       case Token.RSH:
197 |       case Token.URSH:
198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:

214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.NAME:
236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.GETELEM:
240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);

270 |

===== M0150 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0153 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

===== M0165 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 207-237
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
207 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
208 |     if (parent != null) {
209 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
210 |         case Token.DO:
211 |         case Token.FOR:
212 |         case Token.TRY:
213 |         case Token.WHILE:
214 |         case Token.WITH:
215 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
216 |           // TRY
217 |           //   BLOCK
218 |           //   BLOCK
219 |           //     CATCH
220 |           //   BLOCK
221 |           // Note that there is an explicit CATCH token but no explicit
222 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
223 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
224 |           return true;
225 |         case Token.AND:
226 |         case Token.HOOK:
227 |         case Token.IF:
228 |         case Token.OR:
229 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
230 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
231 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
232 | 
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     return n.isCase();
237 |   }

Method code:
543 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
544 |     if (child == first) {
545 |       return null;
546 |     }
547 |     Node n = first;
548 |     while (n.next != child) {
549 |       n = n.next;
550 |       if (n == null) {
551 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
552 |       }
553 |     }
554 |     return n;
555 |   }

807 |   private void validateMaximumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
808 |     boolean valid = false;

Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticeable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource
381 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
382 | 
383 |         Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
384 | 
385 |         // ResourceBundle does caching.
386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {
390 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
391 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
392 |             throw new RuntimeException
393 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
394 |         }
395 | 
396 |         /*
397 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
398 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
399 |          * single 's.
400 |          */
401 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
402 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
403 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
404 |     }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 672-684
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
672 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
673 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
674 |     if (typeSet == null) {
675 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
676 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
680 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
681 | 
682 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
683 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
684 |   }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseAtScope(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 351-372
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.
   */
Method code:
351 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
352 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
353 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
354 |       // We need to do some extra magic to make sure that the scope doesn't
355 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.
356 |       if (inputId == null) {
357 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
358 |       }
359 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
360 |       curNode = n;
361 |       pushScope(s);
362 | 
363 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
364 |       Node body = args.getNext();
365 |       traverseBranch(args, n);
366 |       traverseBranch(body, n);
367 | 
368 |       popScope();
369 |     } else {
370 |       traverseWithScope(n, s);
371 |     }
372 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *

===== M0248 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAnnotation(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 269-1014
Documentation:

Method code:
269 |   private JsDocToken parseAnnotation(JsDocToken token,
270 |       List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
271 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
272 |     JSTypeExpression type;
273 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
274 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
275 | 
276 |     String annotationName = stream.getString();
277 |     Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
278 |     if (annotation == null) {
279 |       parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
280 |           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
281 |     } else {
282 |       // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
283 |       jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
284 | 
285 |       switch (annotation) {
286 |         case NG_INJECT:
287 |           if (jsdocBuilder.isNgInjectRecorded()) {
288 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nginject.extra",
289 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
290 |           } else {
291 |             jsdocBuilder.recordNgInject(true);
292 |           }
293 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
294 | 
295 |         case AUTHOR:
296 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
297 |             ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
298 |             String author = authorInfo.string;
299 | 
300 |             if (author.length() == 0) {
301 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
302 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
303 |             } else {
304 |               jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
305 |             }
306 |             token = authorInfo.token;
307 |           } else {
308 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
309 |           }
310 |           return token;
311 | 
312 |         case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
313 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
314 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
315 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
316 |           }
317 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
318 | 
319 |         case STRUCT:
320 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStruct()) {
321 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
322 |                                   stream.getLineno(),
323 |                                   stream.getCharno());
324 |           }
325 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
326 | 
327 |         case DICT:
328 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDict()) {
329 |             parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
330 |                                   stream.getLineno(),
331 |                                   stream.getCharno());
332 |           }
333 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
334 | 
335 |         case CONSTRUCTOR:
336 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
337 |             if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
338 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
339 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
340 |             } else {
341 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
342 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |             }
344 |           }
345 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
346 | 
347 |         case DEPRECATED:
348 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
349 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
350 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
351 |           }
352 | 
353 |           // Find the reason/description, if any.
354 |           ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
355 |               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
356 | 
357 |           String reason = reasonInfo.string;
358 | 
359 |           if (reason.length() > 0) {
360 |             jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
361 |           }
362 | 
363 |           token = reasonInfo.token;
364 |           return token;
365 | 
366 |         case INTERFACE:
367 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
368 |             if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
369 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
370 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
371 |             } else {
372 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
373 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
374 |             }
375 |           }
376 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
377 | 
378 |         case DESC:
379 |           if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
380 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
381 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
382 |             return eatTokensUntilEOL();
383 |           } else {
384 |             ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
385 |                 extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
386 | 
387 |             String description = descriptionInfo.string;
388 | 
389 |             jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
390 |             token = descriptionInfo.token;
391 |             return token;
392 |           }
393 | 
394 |         case FILE_OVERVIEW:
395 |           String fileOverview = "";
396 |           if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
397 |             ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
398 |                 extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
399 |                     WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
400 | 
401 |             fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
402 | 
403 |             token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
404 |           } else {
405 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
406 |           }
407 | 
408 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview)) {
409 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
410 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
411 |           }
412 |           return token;
413 | 
414 |         case LICENSE:
415 |         case PRESERVE:
416 |           ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
417 |               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
418 |                                            WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
419 | 
420 |           String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
421 | 
422 |           if (preserve.length() > 0) {
423 |             if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
424 |               fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
425 |             }
426 |           }
427 | 
428 |           token = preserveInfo.token;
429 |           return token;
430 | 
431 |         case ENUM:
432 |           token = next();
433 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
434 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
435 | 
436 |           type = null;
437 |           if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
438 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(
439 |                 parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
440 |           }
441 | 
442 |           if (type == null) {
443 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
444 |           }
445 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
446 |             parser.addTypeWarning(
447 |                 "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
448 |           }
449 |           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
450 |           return token;
451 | 
452 |         case EXPORT:
453 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
454 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
455 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
456 |           }
457 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
458 | 
459 |         case EXPOSE:
460 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
461 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
462 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
463 |           }
464 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
465 | 
466 |         case EXTERNS:
467 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
468 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
469 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
470 |           }
471 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
472 | 
473 |         case JAVA_DISPATCH:
474 |           if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
475 |             parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
476 |                 stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
477 |           }
478 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
479 | 
480 |         case EXTENDS:
481 |         case IMPLEMENTS:
482 |           skipEOLs();
483 |           token = next();
484 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
485 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
486 |           boolean matchingRc = false;
487 | 
488 |           if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
489 |             token = next();
490 |             matchingRc = true;
491 |           }
492 | 
493 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
494 |             Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
495 |                 token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
496 | 
497 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
498 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
499 | 
500 |             typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
501 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
502 | 
503 |             if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
504 |               // record the extended type, check later
505 |               extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
506 |                   type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
507 |             } else {
508 |               Preconditions.checkState(
509 |                   annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
510 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
511 |                 parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",

519 |                     stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
520 |               }
521 |             } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
522 |                 token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
523 |               parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
524 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
525 |             }

528 |           }

911 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
912 |             // @type {type}\n@private

/**
     * Rewrite named unhoisted functions declarations to a known
     * consistent behavior so we don't to different logic paths for the same

===== M0255 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 596-611
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
596 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
597 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
598 |       Set<String> directives = null;
599 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
600 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
601 |         if (directives == null) {
602 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
603 |         } else {
604 |           directives.add(directive);
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 | 
608 |       if (directives != null) {
609 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
610 |       }
611 |     }

Method code:
774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);

449 |         if (ref.isLvalue() || ref.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
450 |           if (assignment == null) {
451 |             assignment = ref;
452 |           } else {
453 |             return null;
454 |           }
455 |         }
456 |       }
457 |       return assignment;
458 |     }

   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.

Method code:
502 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
503 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
504 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
505 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
506 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {

513 |         inputs.add(input);
514 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 |     return inputs;
518 |   }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.BasicBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 703-722
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether this block is guaranteed to begin executing before
     * the given block does.
     */
Method code:
703 |     boolean provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock thatBlock) {
704 |       // If thatBlock is a descendant of this block, and there are no hoisted
705 |       // blocks between them, then this block must start before thatBlock.
706 |       BasicBlock currentBlock;
707 |       for (currentBlock = thatBlock;
708 |            currentBlock != null && currentBlock != this;
709 |            currentBlock = currentBlock.getParent()) {
710 |         if (currentBlock.isHoisted) {
711 |           return false;
712 |         }
713 |       }
714 | 
715 |       if (currentBlock == this) {
716 |         return true;
717 |       }
718 |       if (isGlobalScopeBlock() && thatBlock.isGlobalScopeBlock()) {
719 |         return true;
720 |       }
721 |       return false;
722 |     }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1221-1223
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1221 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1222 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1223 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1182-1190
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1182 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1183 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1184 |       return -1;
1185 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1186 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1187 |     } else {
1188 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1189 |     }
1190 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows

   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

===== M0303 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1944-1946
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1944 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1945 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1946 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.checkNamespaces()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 249-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Runs through all namespaces (prefixes of classes and enums), and checks if
   * any of them have been used in an unsafe way.
   */
Method code:
249 |   private void checkNamespaces() {
250 |     for (Name name : nameMap.values()) {
251 |       if (name.isNamespace() &&
252 |           (name.aliasingGets > 0 || name.localSets + name.globalSets > 1 ||
253 |            name.deleteProps > 0)) {
254 |         boolean initialized = name.getDeclaration() != null;
255 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
256 |           if (ref == name.getDeclaration()) {
257 |             continue;
258 |           }
259 | 
260 |           if (ref.type == Ref.Type.DELETE_PROP) {
261 |             if (initialized) {
262 |               warnAboutNamespaceRedefinition(name, ref);
263 |             }
264 |           } else if (
265 |               ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL ||
266 |               ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_LOCAL) {
267 |             if (initialized) {
268 |               warnAboutNamespaceRedefinition(name, ref);
269 |             }
270 | 
271 |             initialized = true;
272 |           } else if (ref.type == Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET) {
273 |             warnAboutNamespaceAliasing(name, ref);
274 |           }
275 |         }
276 |       }
277 |     }
278 |   }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 563-631
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
563 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
564 |     if (this == that) {
565 |       return true;
566 |     }
567 | 
568 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
569 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
570 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
571 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
572 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
573 |         // with everyone.
574 |         return true;
575 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
576 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
577 |         // both unknown.
578 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
579 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
580 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
581 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
582 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
583 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
584 |         return false;
585 |       }
586 |     }
587 | 
588 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
589 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
590 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
591 |     }
592 | 
593 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
594 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
595 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
596 |     }
597 | 
598 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
599 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
600 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
601 |     }
602 | 
603 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
604 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
605 |       return false;
606 |     }
607 | 
608 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
609 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
610 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
611 |     }
612 | 
613 |     // Unbox other proxies.
614 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
615 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
616 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
617 |               that, eqMethod);
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
621 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
622 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
623 |           eqMethod);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
627 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
628 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
629 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
630 |     return this == that;
631 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isWellDefined()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 310-333
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines if the variable for this reference collection is
     * "well-defined." A variable is well-defined if we can prove at
     * compile-time that it's assigned a value before it's used.
     *
     * Notice that if this function returns false, this doesn't imply that the
     * variable is used before it's assigned. It just means that we don't
     * have enough information to make a definitive judgment.
     */
Method code:
310 |     protected boolean isWellDefined() {
311 |       int size = references.size();
312 |       if (size == 0) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // If this is a declaration that does not instantiate the variable,
317 |       // it's not well-defined.
318 |       Reference init = getInitializingReference();
319 |       if (init == null) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       Preconditions.checkState(references.get(0).isDeclaration());
324 |       BasicBlock initBlock = init.getBasicBlock();
325 |       for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
326 |         if (!initBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(
327 |                 references.get(i).getBasicBlock())) {
328 |           return false;
329 |         }
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       return true;
333 |     }

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 159-176
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
160 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
161 |     int thisNumKeys = templateKeys.size();
162 |     int thatNumKeys = that.getTemplateKeys().size();
163 | 
164 |     for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(thisNumKeys, thatNumKeys); i++) {
165 |       JSType thisTemplateType = getTemplateType(templateKeys.get(i));
166 |       JSType thatTemplateType = that.getTemplateType(
167 |           that.getTemplateKeys().get(i));
168 |       if (!thisTemplateType.checkEquivalenceHelper(
169 |           thatTemplateType, eqMethod)) {
170 |         return false;
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     return thisNumKeys == thatNumKeys ||
175 |         eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT;
176 |   }

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 686-705
Documentation:

Method code:
686 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
687 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
688 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
689 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
690 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
691 |       if (typeSet == null) {
692 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
693 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
694 |       }
695 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
696 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
697 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
698 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
699 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
700 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
701 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
702 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
703 |       }
704 |     }
705 |   }

===== M0352 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.NodeFilter.apply(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java

Documentation:

Method code:
196 |     @Override
197 |     public boolean apply(Node n) {
198 |       if (!n.isQualifiedName()) {
199 |         return false;
200 |       }
201 | 
202 |       Node current;
203 |       for (current = n;
204 |            current.isGetProp();
205 |            current = current.getFirstChild()) {
206 |         if (newNodes.contains(current)) {
207 |           return true;
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |

212 |     }
