===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1575-1586
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns whether a node corresponds to a simple or a qualified name, such as
   * <code>x</code> or <code>a.b.c</code> or <code>this.a</code>.
   */
Method code:
1575 |   public boolean isQualifiedName() {
1576 |     switch (getType()) {
1577 |       case Token.NAME:
1578 |         return getString().isEmpty() ? false : true;
1579 |       case Token.THIS:
1580 |         return true;
1581 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1582 |         return getFirstChild().isQualifiedName();
1583 |       default:
1584 |         return false;
1585 |     }
1586 |   }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 103-756
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
104 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     int type = n.getType();
109 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
110 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
111 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
112 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
113 | 
114 |     // Handle all binary operators
115 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(
117 |           childCount == 2,
118 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
119 |           opstr, childCount);
120 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
121 | 
122 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
123 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
124 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
125 | 
126 |       // Handle associativity.
127 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
128 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
129 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
130 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
131 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
132 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
133 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
134 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
135 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
136 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
137 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
138 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
139 |       } else {
140 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
141 |       }
142 |       return;
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
146 | 
147 |     switch (type) {
148 |       case Token.TRY: {
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
150 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
152 | 
153 |         add("try");
154 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
155 | 
156 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
157 |         // isn't a catch block
158 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
159 |         if (catchblock != null) {
160 |           add(catchblock);
161 |         }
162 | 
163 |         if (childCount == 3) {
164 |           add("finally");
165 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
166 |         }
167 |         break;
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       case Token.CATCH:
171 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
172 |         add("catch(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
242 |         break;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.NUMBER:
245 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
246 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
247 |         break;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
250 |       case Token.VOID:
251 |       case Token.NOT:
252 |       case Token.BITNOT:
253 |       case Token.POS: {
254 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
255 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
257 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEG: {
262 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
263 | 
264 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
265 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
266 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
267 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
268 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
269 |         } else {
270 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
271 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         break;
275 |       }
276 | 
277 |       case Token.HOOK: {
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
280 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
281 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
282 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
283 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
284 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
285 |         break;
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       case Token.REGEXP:
289 |         if (!first.isString() ||
290 |             !last.isString()) {
291 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
292 |         }
293 | 
294 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
295 | 
296 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
297 |         if (childCount == 2) {
298 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
299 |         } else {
300 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
301 |           add(regexp);
302 |         }
303 |         break;
304 | 
305 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
306 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
307 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
308 |         }
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
310 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
311 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
312 |           add("(");
313 |         }
314 | 
315 |         add("function");
316 |         add(first);
317 | 
318 |         add(first.getNext());
319 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
320 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
321 | 
322 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
323 |           add(")");
324 |         }
325 |         break;
326 | 
327 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
328 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
329 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
330 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
332 | 
333 |         // Get methods are unnamed
334 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
335 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
336 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
337 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
338 |           add("get ");
339 |         } else {
340 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
341 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
342 |           add("set ");
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
346 |         String name = n.getString();
347 |         Node fn = first;
348 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
349 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
350 | 
351 |         // Add the property name.
352 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
353 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
354 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
355 |             // Unicode escaped.
356 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
357 |           add(name);
358 |         } else {
359 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
360 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
361 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
362 |             cc.addNumber(d);
363 |           } else {
364 |             addJsString(n);
365 |           }
366 |         }
367 | 
368 |         add(parameters);
369 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
373 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
374 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
375 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
376 |         }
377 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
378 |         if (preserveBlock) {
379 |           cc.beginBlock();
380 |         }
381 | 
382 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
383 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
384 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
385 |                 !preserveBlock &&
386 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
387 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
388 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
389 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
390 | 
391 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
392 |           if (c.isVar()) {
393 |             cc.endStatement();
394 |           }
395 | 
396 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
397 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
398 |           }
399 | 
400 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
401 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
402 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
403 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
404 |           }
405 |         }
406 |         if (preserveBlock) {
407 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
408 |         }
409 |         break;
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       case Token.FOR:
413 |         if (childCount == 4) {
414 |           add("for(");
415 |           if (first.isVar()) {
416 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
417 |           } else {
418 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
419 |           }
420 |           add(";");
421 |           add(first.getNext());
422 |           add(";");
423 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         } else {
428 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
429 |           add("for(");
430 |           add(first);
431 |           add("in");
432 |           add(first.getNext());
433 |           add(")");
434 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         }
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.DO:
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
441 |         add("do");
442 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(last);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         cc.endStatement();
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.WHILE:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
451 |         add("while(");
452 |         add(first);
453 |         add(")");
454 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
455 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.EMPTY:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
466 |         Preconditions.checkState(
467 |             last.isString(),
468 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
469 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
470 |         if (needsParens) {
471 |           add("(");
472 |         }
473 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
474 |         if (needsParens) {
475 |           add(")");
476 |         }
477 |         add(".");
478 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
479 |         break;
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       case Token.GETELEM:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(
484 |             childCount == 2,
485 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
486 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |         add("[");
488 |         add(first.getNext());
489 |         add("]");
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.WITH:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
494 |         add("with(");
495 |         add(first);
496 |         add(")");
497 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
498 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.INC:
502 |       case Token.DEC: {
503 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
504 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
505 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
506 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
507 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
508 |         if (postProp != 0) {
509 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
510 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
511 |         } else {
512 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
513 |           add(first);
514 |         }
515 |         break;
516 |       }
517 | 
518 |       case Token.CALL:
519 |         // We have two special cases here:
520 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
521 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
522 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
523 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
524 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
525 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
526 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
527 |         // that must be preserved.
528 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
529 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
530 |           add("(0,");
531 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
532 |           add(")");
533 |         } else {
534 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
535 |         }
536 |         add("(");
537 |         addList(first.getNext());
538 |         add(")");
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.IF:
542 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
543 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
544 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
545 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
546 |           cc.beginBlock();
547 |         }
548 | 
549 |         add("if(");
550 |         add(first);
551 |         add(")");
552 | 
553 |         if (hasElse) {
554 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
555 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
556 |           add("else");
557 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
558 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
559 |         } else {
560 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
561 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
562 |         }
563 | 
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.endBlock();
566 |         }
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NULL:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
571 |         cc.addConstant("null");
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.THIS:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("this");
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.FALSE:
580 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
581 |         cc.addConstant("false");
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.TRUE:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         cc.addConstant("true");
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
591 |         add("continue");
592 |         if (childCount == 1) {
593 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
594 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
595 |           }
596 |           add(" ");
597 |           add(first);
598 |         }
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
603 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
604 |         add("debugger");
605 |         cc.endStatement();
606 |         break;
607 | 
608 |       case Token.BREAK:
609 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
610 |         add("break");
611 |         if (childCount == 1) {
612 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
613 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
614 |           }
615 |           add(" ");
616 |           add(first);
617 |         }
618 |         cc.endStatement();
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
624 |         cc.endStatement();
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.NEW:
628 |         add("new ");
629 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
630 | 
631 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
632 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
633 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
634 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
635 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
636 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
637 |         }
638 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
639 | 
640 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
641 |         Node next = first.getNext();
642 |         if (next != null) {
643 |           add("(");
644 |           addList(next);
645 |           add(")");
646 |         }
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
650 |         Preconditions.checkState(
651 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
652 |         addJsString(n);
653 |         break;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.STRING:
656 |         Preconditions.checkState(
657 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
658 |         addJsString(n);
659 |         break;
660 | 
661 |       case Token.DELPROP:
662 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
663 |         add("delete ");
664 |         add(first);
665 |         break;
666 | 
667 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
668 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
669 |         if (needsParens) {
670 |           add("(");
671 |         }
672 |         add("{");
673 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
674 |           if (c != first) {
675 |             cc.listSeparator();
676 |           }
677 | 
678 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
679 |             add(c);
680 |           } else {
681 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
682 |             String key = c.getString();
683 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
684 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
685 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
686 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
687 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
688 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
689 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
690 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
691 |               add(key);
692 |             } else {
693 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
694 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
695 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
696 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
697 |               } else {
698 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
699 |               }
700 |             }
701 |             add(":");
702 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
703 |           }
704 |         }
705 |         add("}");
706 |         if (needsParens) {
707 |           add(")");
708 |         }
709 |         break;
710 |       }
711 | 
712 |       case Token.SWITCH:
713 |         add("switch(");
714 |         add(first);
715 |         add(")");
716 |         cc.beginBlock();
717 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
718 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
723 |         add("case ");
724 |         add(first);
725 |         addCaseBody(last);
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
729 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
730 |         add("default");
731 |         addCaseBody(first);
732 |         break;
733 | 
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
736 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
737 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
738 |         }
739 |         add(first);
740 |         add(":");
741 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
742 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
743 |         break;
744 | 
745 |       case Token.CAST:
746 |         add("(");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         add(")");
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       default:
752 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
753 |     }
754 | 
755 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
756 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.inlineAliasIfPossible(Ref,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 199-243
Documentation:

Method code:
199 |   private boolean inlineAliasIfPossible(Ref alias, GlobalNamespace namespace) {
200 |     // Ensure that the alias is assigned to a local variable at that
201 |     // variable's declaration. If the alias's parent is a NAME,
202 |     // then the NAME must be the child of a VAR node, and we must
203 |     // be in a VAR assignment.
204 |     Node aliasParent = alias.node.getParent();
205 |     if (aliasParent.isName()) {
206 |       // Ensure that the local variable is well defined and never reassigned.
207 |       Scope scope = alias.scope;
208 |       Var aliasVar = scope.getVar(aliasParent.getString());
209 |       ReferenceCollectingCallback collector =
210 |           new ReferenceCollectingCallback(compiler,
211 |               ReferenceCollectingCallback.DO_NOTHING_BEHAVIOR,
212 |               Predicates.<Var>equalTo(aliasVar));
213 |       (new NodeTraversal(compiler, collector)).traverseAtScope(scope);
214 | 
215 |       ReferenceCollection aliasRefs = collector.getReferences(aliasVar);
216 |       if (aliasRefs.isWellDefined()
217 |           && aliasRefs.firstReferenceIsAssigningDeclaration()
218 |           && aliasRefs.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
219 |         // The alias is well-formed, so do the inlining now.
220 |         int size = aliasRefs.references.size();
221 |         Set<Node> newNodes = Sets.newHashSetWithExpectedSize(size - 1);
222 |         for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
223 |           ReferenceCollectingCallback.Reference aliasRef =
224 |               aliasRefs.references.get(i);
225 | 
226 |           Node newNode = alias.node.cloneTree();
227 |           aliasRef.getParent().replaceChild(aliasRef.getNode(), newNode);
228 |           newNodes.add(newNode);
229 |         }
230 | 
231 |         // just set the original alias to null.
232 |         aliasParent.replaceChild(alias.node, IR.nullNode());
233 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
234 | 
235 |         // Inlining the variable may have introduced new references
236 |         // to descendants of {@code name}. So those need to be collected now.
237 |         namespace.scanNewNodes(alias.scope, newNodes);
238 |         return true;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return false;
243 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1495-1544
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1495 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1496 |     if (type != node.getType()
1497 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1498 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1499 |       return false;
1500 |     }
1501 | 
1502 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1503 |       return false;
1504 |     }
1505 | 
1506 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1507 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1508 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1509 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1510 |         return false;
1511 |       }
1512 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1513 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1514 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1515 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1516 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1517 |           return false;
1518 |         }
1519 |       }
1520 | 
1521 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1522 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1523 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1524 |         return false;
1525 |       }
1526 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1527 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1528 |         return false;
1529 |       }
1530 |     }
1531 | 
1532 |     if (recurse) {
1533 |       Node n, n2;
1534 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1535 |            n != null;
1536 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1537 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1538 |           return false;
1539 |         }
1540 |       }
1541 |     }
1542 | 
1543 |     return true;
1544 |   }

105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 752-834
Documentation:

Method code:
752 |     @Override
753 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
754 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
755 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
756 |       if (name == null) {
757 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
758 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
759 |           errorReporter.error(
760 |             "unnamed function statement",
761 |             sourceName,
762 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
763 | 
764 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
765 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
766 |         }
767 |         name = new Name();
768 |         name.setIdentifier("");
769 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
770 |       }
771 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
772 |       Node newName = transform(name);
773 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
774 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
775 |         // declaration.
776 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
777 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
778 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
779 |         // same line as the function keyword.
780 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
781 |             functionNode.getLp();
782 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
783 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
784 |       }
785 | 
786 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
787 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
788 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
789 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
790 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
791 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
792 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
793 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
794 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
795 |       if (fnName != null) {
796 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
797 |       } else {
798 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
799 |       }
800 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
801 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
802 | 
803 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
804 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
805 |         Node paramNode = transformParameter(param);
806 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
807 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
808 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
809 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
810 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
811 |         } else {
812 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
813 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
814 |           // (an error has already been reported).
815 |           Preconditions.checkState(
816 |               config.isIdeMode
817 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
818 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
819 |         }
820 |       }
821 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
822 | 
823 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
824 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
825 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
826 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
827 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
828 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
829 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
830 |       }
831 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
832 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
833 |      return node;
834 |     }

===== M0373 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 |

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.Name.addRef(Ref)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 931-962

Method code:
931 |     void addRef(Ref ref) {
932 |       addRefInternal(ref);
933 |       switch (ref.type) {
934 |         case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
935 |           if (declaration == null) {
936 |             declaration = ref;
937 |             docInfo = getDocInfoForDeclaration(ref);
938 |           }
939 |           globalSets++;
940 |           break;
941 |         case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
942 |           localSets++;
943 |           break;
944 |         case PROTOTYPE_GET:
945 |         case DIRECT_GET:
946 |           totalGets++;
947 |           break;
948 |         case ALIASING_GET:
949 |           aliasingGets++;
950 |           totalGets++;
951 |           break;
952 |         case CALL_GET:
953 |           callGets++;
954 |           totalGets++;
955 |           break;
956 |         case DELETE_PROP:
957 |           deleteProps++;
958 |           break;
959 |         default:
960 |           throw new IllegalStateException();
961 |       }
962 |     }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(Name,String,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 930-958
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds global variable "stubs" for any properties of a global name that are
   * only set in a local scope or read but never set.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name of the object whose properties we are
   *     adding stubs for (e.g. "a$b$c")
   * @param parent The node to which new global variables should be added
   *     as children
   * @param addAfter The child of after which new
   *     variables should be added (may be null)
   * @return The number of variables added
   */
Method code:
930 |   private int addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(
931 |       Name n, String alias, Node parent, Node addAfter) {
932 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames());
933 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent));
934 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(addAfter);
935 |     int numStubs = 0;
936 |     if (n.props != null) {
937 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
938 |         if (p.needsToBeStubbed()) {
939 |           String propAlias = appendPropForAlias(alias, p.getBaseName());
940 |           Node nameNode = IR.name(propAlias);
941 |           Node newVar = IR.var(nameNode)
942 |               .copyInformationFromForTree(addAfter);
943 |           parent.addChildAfter(newVar, addAfter);
944 |           addAfter = newVar;
945 |           numStubs++;
946 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
947 | 
948 |           // Determine if this is a constant var by checking the first
949 |           // reference to it. Don't check the declaration, as it might be null.
950 |           if (p.getRefs().get(0).node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(
951 |                   Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
952 |             nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
953 |           }
954 |         }
955 |       }
956 |     }
957 |     return numStubs;
958 |   }

===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 497-522
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapses definitions of the collapsible properties of a global name.
   * Recurses on subnames that also represent JavaScript objects with
   * collapsible properties.
   *
   * @param n A node representing a global name
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n}
   */
Method code:
497 |   private void collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(Name n, String alias) {
498 |     boolean canCollapseChildNames = n.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames();
499 | 
500 |     // Handle this name first so that nested object literals get unrolled.
501 |     if (n.canCollapse()) {
502 |       updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(n, alias, canCollapseChildNames);
503 |     }
504 | 
505 |     if (n.props != null) {
506 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
507 |         // Recurse first so that saved node ancestries are intact when needed.
508 |         collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(
509 |             p, appendPropForAlias(alias, p.getBaseName()));
510 | 
511 |         if (!p.inExterns && canCollapseChildNames &&
512 |             p.getDeclaration() != null &&
513 |             p.canCollapse() &&
514 |             p.getDeclaration().node != null &&
515 |             p.getDeclaration().node.getParent() != null &&
516 |             p.getDeclaration().node.getParent().isAssign()) {
517 |           updateSimpleDeclaration(
518 |               appendPropForAlias(alias, p.getBaseName()), p, p.getDeclaration());
519 |         }
520 |       }
521 |     }
522 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();

186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;

188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 549-569
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
549 |   @Override
550 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
551 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
552 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
553 |     if (unknown) {
554 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
555 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
556 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
557 |         unknown = false;
558 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
559 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
560 |             unknown = true;
561 |             break;
562 |           }
563 |         }
564 |       } else {
565 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
566 |       }
567 |     }
568 |     return unknown;
569 |   }

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,CompilerOptions,JSTypeRegistry,SourceMap,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 654-691
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
654 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
655 |       CompilerOptions options, JSTypeRegistry registry,
656 |       SourceMap sourceMap,  boolean tagAsStrict) {
657 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
658 | 
659 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
660 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
661 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
662 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
663 |             options.lineBreak,
664 |             options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile,
665 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,

668 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
669 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
670 |             createSourceMap,
671 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel);
672 |     CodeGenerator cg =
673 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED

683 |

688 |     }
689 |

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 121-160
Documentation:

Method code:
121 |   @Override
122 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
123 |     new NodeTraversal(
124 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
125 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
126 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
127 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
128 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
129 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
130 |     }
131 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
132 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
133 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
134 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
135 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
136 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
137 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
138 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
139 |     //   function f() {
140 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
141 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
142 |     //   }
143 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
144 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
145 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
146 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
147 |         .process(externs, root);
148 | 
149 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
150 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
151 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
152 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
153 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
154 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
158 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
159 |     }
160 |   }

===== M0428 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 647-679
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
647 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
648 |       Preconditions.checkState(
649 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
650 |       Node previous = null;
651 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
652 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
653 |       // are already in the right place.
654 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
655 |         previous = current;
656 |         current = current.getNext();
657 |       }
658 | 
659 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
660 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
661 |       while (current != null) {
662 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
663 |         Node next = current.getNext();
664 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
665 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
666 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
667 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
668 | 
669 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
670 |           // previous declarations).
671 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
672 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
673 |         } else {
674 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
675 |           previous = current;
676 |         }
677 |         current = next;
678 |       }
679 |     }

Method code:
464 |     @Override
465 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
466 |       if (lineBreak) {
467 |         if (sawFunction) {
468 |           startNewLine();
469 |           sawFunction = false;
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the

480 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |       maybeCutLine();
484 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 1054-1084
Documentation:

Method code:
1054 |     boolean canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames() {
1055 |       if (type == Type.OTHER || isGetOrSetDefinition()
1056 |           || globalSets != 1 || localSets != 0 || deleteProps != 0) {
1057 |         return false;
1058 |       }
1059 | 
1060 |       // Don't try to collapse if the one global set is a twin reference.
1061 |       // We could theoretically handle this case in CollapseProperties, but
1062 |       // it's probably not worth the effort.
1063 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(declaration);
1064 |       if (declaration.getTwin() != null) {
1065 |         return false;
1066 |       }
1067 | 
1068 |       if (declaredType) {
1069 |         return true;
1070 |       }
1071 | 
1072 |       // If this is a key of an aliased object literal, then it will be aliased
1073 |       // later. So we won't be able to collapse its properties.
1074 |       if (parent != null && parent.shouldKeepKeys()) {
1075 |         return false;
1076 |       }
1077 | 
1078 |       // If this is aliased, then its properties can't be collapsed either.
1079 |       if (aliasingGets > 0) {
1080 |         return false;
1081 |       }
1082 | 
1083 |       return (parent == null || parent.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames());
1084 |     }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 624-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
624 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
625 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
626 |         next = c.getNext();
627 |         if (c.isVar()) {
628 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
629 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
630 |           }
631 | 
632 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
633 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
634 |             c.removeChild(name);
635 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
636 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
637 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
638 |           }
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }

===== M0443 =====

Original range: 790-805
Documentation:

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |       root = result.ast;
91 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(
94 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = IR.script();
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
105 |     // the source file and module.
106 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
107 |   }

179 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,

194 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1431-1450
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1431 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1432 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1433 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1434 |     }
1435 | 
1436 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1437 |     Node n, n2;
1438 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1439 |          res == null && n != null;
1440 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1441 |       if (node2 == null) {
1442 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1443 |       }
1444 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1445 |       if (res != null) {
1446 |         return res;
1447 |       }
1448 |     }
1449 |     return res;
1450 |   }

===== M0507 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 935-994
Documentation:

Method code:
935 |   private void toString(
936 |       StringBuilder sb,
937 |       boolean printSource,
938 |       boolean printAnnotations,
939 |       boolean printType) {
940 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
941 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
942 |       sb.append(' ');
943 |       sb.append(getString());
944 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
945 |       sb.append(' ');
946 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
947 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
948 |       // toStringTree.
949 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
950 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
951 |       } else {
952 |         sb.append(first.getString());
953 |       }
954 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getDouble());
957 |     }
958 |     if (printSource) {
959 |       int lineno = getLineno();
960 |       if (lineno != -1) {
961 |         sb.append(' ');
962 |         sb.append(lineno);
963 |       }
964 |     }
965 | 
966 |     if (printAnnotations) {
967 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
968 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
969 |         int type = keys[i];
970 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
971 |         sb.append(" [");
972 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
973 |         sb.append(": ");
974 |         String value;
975 |         switch (type) {
976 |           default:
977 |             value = x.toString();
978 |             break;
979 |         }
980 |         sb.append(value);
981 |         sb.append(']');
982 |       }
983 |     }
984 | 
985 |     if (printType) {
986 |       if (jsType != null) {
987 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
988 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
989 |           sb.append(" : ");
990 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
991 |         }
992 |       }
993 |     }
994 |   }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 111-154
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
111 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
112 |                                   String sourceString,
113 |                                   Config config,
114 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
115 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
116 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
117 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
118 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
119 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
120 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
121 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
123 | 
124 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
125 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
126 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
127 | 
128 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
129 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
130 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
131 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
132 | 
133 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
134 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
135 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
136 | 
137 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
138 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
139 |     try {
140 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
141 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
142 |       logger.info(
143 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
144 |     } finally {
145 |       Context.exit();
146 |     }
147 |     Node root = null;
148 |     if (astRoot != null) {
149 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
150 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
151 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
152 |     }
153 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
154 |   }

===== M0529 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2429-2438
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2429 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2430 |     int len = s.length();
2431 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2432 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2433 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2434 |         return false;
2435 |       }
2436 |     }
2437 |     return true;
2438 |   }

===== M0533 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 310-349
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
310 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
311 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
312 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
313 |       options.checkTypes = true;
314 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
315 |       options.checkTypes = false;
316 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
317 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
318 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
319 |       // parser type warnings.
320 |       options.setWarningLevel(
321 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
322 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
323 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
324 |     }
325 | 
326 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
327 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
328 |       options.setWarningLevel(
329 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
330 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
331 |     }
332 | 
333 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
334 |       options.setWarningLevel(
335 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
336 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
337 |     }
338 | 
339 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
340 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
341 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
342 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
343 |     // error off.
344 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
345 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
346 |       options.setWarningLevel(
347 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
348 |     }
349 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 769-807
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
769 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
770 |         String name) {
771 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
772 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
773 |       // assignments.
774 | 
775 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
776 |       String prefix;
777 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
778 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
779 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
780 |       } else {
781 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
782 |         if (i == -1) {
783 |           return false;
784 |         }
785 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
786 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
787 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
788 |         while (i >= 0) {
789 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
790 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
791 |         }
792 |       }
793 | 
794 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
795 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
796 |         // key, so we're done.
797 |         return true;
798 |       }
799 | 
800 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
801 |         parent = n;
802 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
803 |       }
804 | 
805 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
806 |       return true;
807 |     }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1229-1273
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1229 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1230 |     // unknown
1231 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1232 |       return true;
1233 |     }
1234 |     // all type
1235 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1236 |       return true;
1237 |     }
1238 |     // equality
1239 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // unions
1243 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1244 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1245 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1246 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1247 |           return true;
1248 |         }
1249 |       }
1250 |       return false;
1251 |     }
1252 | 
1253 |     // templatized types.
1254 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1255 |       return !areIncompatibleArrays(thisType, thatType) &&
1256 |           thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1257 |               thatType);
1258 |     }
1259 |     if (thatType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1260 |       if (!isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType) &&
1261 |           !thisType.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
1262 |               thatType.getTemplateTypeMap(), EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
1263 |         return false;
1264 |       }
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     // proxy types
1268 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1269 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1270 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1271 |     }
1272 |     return false;
1273 |   }
