===== M0607 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 161-197
Documentation:
/**
   * For each qualified name N in the global scope, we check if:
   * (a) No ancestor of N is ever aliased or assigned an unknown value type.
   *     (If N = "a.b.c", "a" and "a.b" are never aliased).
   * (b) N has exactly one write, and it lives in the global scope.
   * (c) N is aliased in a local scope.
   *
   * If (a) is true, then GlobalNamespace must know all the writes to N.
   * If (a) and (b) are true, then N cannot change during the execution of
   *    a local scope.
   * If (a) and (b) and (c) are true, then the alias can be inlined if the
   *    alias obeys the usual rules for how we decide whether a variable is
   *    inlineable.
   * @see InlineVariables
   */
Method code:
161 |   private void inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace namespace) {
162 |     // Invariant: All the names in the worklist meet condition (a).
163 |     Deque<Name> workList = new ArrayDeque<Name>(namespace.getNameForest());
164 |     while (!workList.isEmpty()) {
165 |       Name name = workList.pop();
166 | 
167 |       // Don't attempt to inline a getter or setter property as a variable.
168 |       if (name.type == Name.Type.GET || name.type == Name.Type.SET) {
169 |         continue;
170 |       }
171 | 
172 |       if (name.globalSets == 1 && name.localSets == 0 &&
173 |           name.aliasingGets > 0) {
174 |         // {@code name} meets condition (b). Find all of its local aliases
175 |         // and try to inline them.
176 |         List<Ref> refs = Lists.newArrayList(name.getRefs());
177 |         for (Ref ref : refs) {
178 |           if (ref.type == Type.ALIASING_GET && ref.scope.isLocal()) {
179 |             // {@code name} meets condition (c). Try to inline it.
180 |             if (inlineAliasIfPossible(ref, namespace)) {
181 |               name.removeRef(ref);
182 |             }
183 |           }
184 |         }
185 |       }
186 | 
187 |       // Check if {@code name} has any aliases left after the
188 |       // local-alias-inlining above.
189 |       if ((name.type == Name.Type.OBJECTLIT ||
190 |            name.type == Name.Type.FUNCTION) &&
191 |           name.aliasingGets == 0 && name.props != null) {
192 |         // All of {@code name}'s children meet condition (a), so they can be
193 |         // added to the worklist.
194 |         workList.addAll(name.props);
195 |       }
196 |     }
197 |   }

Method code:
299 |     @Override
300 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
301 |       if (n.isName()) {
302 |         String name = n.getString();
303 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
304 |           return;
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
308 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
309 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
310 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
311 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
312 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
313 |             expectedConst = true;
314 |           } else {
315 |             expectedConst = false;
316 | 
317 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
318 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
319 |             if (var != null) {
320 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
321 |             }
322 | 
323 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
324 |               expectedConst = true;
325 |             } else {
326 |               expectedConst = false;
327 |             }
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           if (expectedConst) {
331 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
332 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
333 |           } else {
334 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
335 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
336 |           }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
340 |         if (value == null) {
341 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
342 |         } else {
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
344 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     }

119 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {

===== M0656 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleGet(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 631-681
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our representation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     */
Method code:
631 |     void handleGet(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name) {
632 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
633 | 
634 |       Ref.Type type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
635 |       if (parent != null) {
636 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
637 |           case Token.IF:
638 |           case Token.TYPEOF:
639 |           case Token.VOID:
640 |           case Token.NOT:
641 |           case Token.BITNOT:
642 |           case Token.POS:
643 |           case Token.NEG:
644 |             break;
645 |           case Token.CALL:
646 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
647 |                    ? Ref.Type.CALL_GET
648 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
649 |             break;
650 |           case Token.NEW:
651 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
652 |                    ? Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET
653 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
654 |             break;
655 |           case Token.OR:
656 |           case Token.AND:
657 |             // This node is x or y in (x||y) or (x&&y). We only know that an
658 |             // alias is not getting created for this name if the result is used
659 |             // in a boolean context or assigned to the same name
660 |             // (e.g. var a = a || {}).
661 |             type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
662 |             break;
663 |           case Token.HOOK:
664 |             if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
665 |               // This node is y or z in (x?y:z). We only know that an alias is
666 |               // not getting created for this name if the result is assigned to
667 |               // the same name (e.g. var a = a ? a : {}).
668 |               type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
669 |             }
670 |             break;
671 |           case Token.DELPROP:
672 |             type = Ref.Type.DELETE_PROP;
673 |             break;
674 |           default:
675 |             type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
676 |             break;
677 |         }
678 |       }
679 | 
680 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, name, type);
681 |     }

===== M0670 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3034-3062
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3034 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3035 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3036 |     if (info == null) {
3037 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3038 |       if (parent == null) {
3039 |         return null;
3040 |       }
3041 | 
3042 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3043 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3044 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3045 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3046 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3047 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3048 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3049 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3050 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3051 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3052 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3053 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3054 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3055 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3056 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3057 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3058 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3059 |       }
3060 |     }
3061 |     return info;
3062 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1169-1244
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1169 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1170 |     switch (type) {
1171 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1183 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1184 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1185 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1186 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1187 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1188 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1189 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1190 |       case Token.EQ:
1191 |       case Token.NE:
1192 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1193 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1194 |       case Token.LT:
1195 |       case Token.GT:
1196 |       case Token.LE:
1197 |       case Token.GE:
1198 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1199 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1200 |       case Token.LSH:
1201 |       case Token.RSH:
1202 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1203 |       case Token.SUB:
1204 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1205 |       case Token.MUL:
1206 |       case Token.MOD:
1207 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1208 |       case Token.INC:
1209 |       case Token.DEC:
1210 |       case Token.NEW:
1211 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1212 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1213 |       case Token.VOID:
1214 |       case Token.NOT:
1215 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1216 |       case Token.POS:
1217 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1218 | 
1219 |       case Token.CALL:
1220 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1221 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1222 |       // Data values
1223 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1224 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1225 |       case Token.FALSE:
1226 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1227 |       case Token.NAME:
1228 |       case Token.NULL:
1229 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1230 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1231 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1232 |       case Token.STRING:
1233 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1234 |       case Token.THIS:
1235 |       case Token.TRUE:
1236 |         return 15;
1237 |       case Token.CAST:
1238 |         return 16;
1239 | 
1240 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1241 |                                Token.name(type) +
1242 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1243 |     }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 222-267
Documentation:

Method code:
222 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
223 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
224 |     while (true) {
225 |       switch (token) {
226 |         case ANNOTATION:
227 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
228 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
229 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
230 |           } else {
231 |             token = next();
232 |           }
233 |           break;
234 | 
235 |         case EOC:
236 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
237 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
238 |           }
239 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
240 |           return true;
241 | 
242 |         case EOF:
243 |           // discard any accumulated information
244 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
245 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
246 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
247 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
248 |           return false;
249 | 
250 |         case EOL:
251 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
252 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
253 |           }
254 |           token = next();
255 |           break;
256 | 
257 |         default:
258 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
259 |             token = next();
260 |           } else {
261 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
262 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
263 |           }
264 |           break;
265 |       }
266 |     }
267 |   }

495 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for

   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }

===== M0698 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1296-1416
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1296 |   Node parseInputs() {
1297 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1298 | 
1299 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1300 |     // individual file parse trees.
1301 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1302 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1303 |     }
1304 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1305 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1306 |     }
1307 | 
1308 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1309 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1310 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1311 | 
1312 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1313 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1314 | 
1315 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1316 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1317 | 
1318 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1319 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1320 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1321 |     }
1322 | 
1323 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1324 | 
1325 |     try {
1326 |       // Parse externs sources.
1327 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1328 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1329 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1330 |           return null;
1331 |         }
1332 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1333 |       }
1334 | 
1335 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1336 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1337 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1338 |       }
1339 | 
1340 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1341 | 
1342 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1343 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1344 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1345 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1346 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1347 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1348 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1349 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1350 |           }
1351 |         }
1352 | 
1353 |         try {
1354 |           inputs =
1355 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1356 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1357 |           staleInputs = true;
1358 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1359 |           report(JSError.make(
1360 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1361 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1362 |           report(JSError.make(
1363 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1364 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1365 |           report(JSError.make(
1366 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1367 |         }
1368 | 
1369 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1370 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1371 |           return null;
1372 |         }
1373 |       }
1374 | 
1375 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1376 | 
1377 |       if (staleInputs) {
1378 |         repartitionInputs();
1379 |       }
1380 | 
1381 |       // Build the AST.
1382 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1383 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1384 |         if (n == null) {
1385 |           continue;
1386 |         }
1387 | 
1388 |         if (devMode) {
1389 |           runSanityCheck();
1390 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1391 |             return null;
1392 |           }
1393 |         }
1394 | 
1395 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1396 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1397 | 
1398 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1399 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1400 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1401 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1402 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1403 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1404 |         }
1405 | 
1406 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1407 |       }
1408 | 
1409 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1410 |         return null;
1411 |       }
1412 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1413 |     } finally {
1414 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1415 |     }
1416 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *

   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array

1300 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {

/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}
   */
Method code:
124 |   public JSDocInfo build(Node associatedNode) {
125 |     if (populated) {
126 |       JSDocInfo built = currentInfo;
127 |       built.setAssociatedNode(associatedNode);
128 |       populateDefaults(built);
129 |       populated = false;
130 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
131 |       return built;
132 |     } else {
133 |       return null;
134 |     }
135 |   }

   * Gets the ancestor node relative to this.
   *
   * @param level 0 = this, 1 = the parent, etc.
   */
Method code:
1319 |   public Node getAncestor(int level) {
1320 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(level >= 0);
1321 |     Node node = this;

Documentation:

Method code:
389 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
390 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
391 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
392 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
393 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
394 |       // a child.
395 |       validateNodeType(Token.NAME, c);
396 |       validateNonEmptyString(c);
397 |       validateMaximumChildCount(c, 1);
398 |       if (c.hasChildren()) {
399 |         validateExpression(c.getFirstChild());
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 |   }

===== M0728 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

Method code:
54 |   @Override
55 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
56 |     if (checkOnly) {
57 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
58 |     } else {
59 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as
60 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
61 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
62 |       // performed.
63 |       if (externs != null) {
64 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
65 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations());
66 |       }
67 |       if (root != null) {
68 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
69 |             compiler, root, new PrepareAnnotations());
70 |       }
71 |     }
72 |   }

===== M0750 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0762 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 289-340
Documentation:

Method code:
289 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
290 |     if (info.hasType()) {
291 |       boolean valid = false;
292 |       switch (node.getType()) {
293 |         // Casts are valid
294 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
295 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
296 |           break;
297 |         // Variable declarations are valid
298 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
299 |           valid = true;
300 |           break;
301 |         // Function declarations are valid
302 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
303 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode)node;
304 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
305 |           break;
306 |         // Object literal properties and catch declarations are valid.
307 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
308 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty
309 |               || node.getParent() instanceof CatchClause
310 |               || node.getParent() instanceof FunctionNode;
311 |           break;
312 |         // Object literal properties are valid
313 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
315 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
317 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
318 |           break;
319 | 
320 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
321 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
322 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
323 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
324 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment)node).getLeft());
325 |           }
326 |           break;
327 | 
328 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
329 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
330 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
331 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
332 |           break;
333 |       }
334 |       if (!valid) {
335 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
336 |             sourceName,
337 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
338 |       }
339 |     }
340 |   }

===== M0813 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0822 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistNoCompileFiles()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1462-1482
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1462 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1463 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1464 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1465 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1466 | 
1467 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1468 |       if (n == null) {
1469 |         continue;
1470 |       }
1471 | 
1472 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1473 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1474 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1475 |         staleInputs = true;
1476 |       }
1477 |     }
1478 | 
1479 |     if (staleInputs) {
1480 |       repartitionInputs();
1481 |     }
1482 |   }

===== M0831 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1421-1457
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1421 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1422 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1423 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1424 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1425 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1426 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1427 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1428 |           continue;
1429 |         }
1430 |       }
1431 | 
1432 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1433 | 
1434 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1435 |       if (n == null) {
1436 |         continue;
1437 |       }
1438 | 
1439 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1440 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1441 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1442 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1443 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1444 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1445 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1446 | 
1447 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1448 | 
1449 |         externs.add(input);
1450 |         staleInputs = true;
1451 |       }
1452 |     }
1453 | 
1454 |     if (staleInputs) {
1455 |       repartitionInputs();
1456 |     }
1457 |   }

===== M0836 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.Name.removeRef(Ref)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 964-1004
Documentation:

Method code:
964 |     void removeRef(Ref ref) {
965 |       if (refs != null && refs.remove(ref)) {
966 |         if (ref == declaration) {
967 |           declaration = null;
968 |           if (refs != null) {
969 |             for (Ref maybeNewDecl : refs) {
970 |               if (maybeNewDecl.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL) {
971 |                 declaration = maybeNewDecl;
972 |                 break;
973 |               }
974 |             }
975 |           }
976 |         }
977 | 
978 |         switch (ref.type) {
979 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
980 |             globalSets--;
981 |             break;
982 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
983 |             localSets--;
984 |             break;
985 |           case PROTOTYPE_GET:
986 |           case DIRECT_GET:
987 |             totalGets--;
988 |             break;
989 |           case ALIASING_GET:
990 |             aliasingGets--;
991 |             totalGets--;
992 |             break;
993 |           case CALL_GET:
994 |             callGets--;
995 |             totalGets--;
996 |             break;
997 |           case DELETE_PROP:
998 |             deleteProps--;
999 |             break;
1000 |           default:
1001 |             throw new IllegalStateException();
1002 |         }
1003 |       }
1004 |     }
